Revert "Reland "ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions""

This reverts commit ff9c8d4945e0d6baab644fa395cf812e9c2ce44d.

Reason for revert: Causing ActorSkipObservationDelayTest.WaitsByDefault to fail on "ci / Linux ChromiumOS MSan Tests" bot.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Linux%20ChromiumOS%20MSan%20Tests/61862/overview

Original change's description:
> Reland "ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions"
>
> This is a reland of commit 51de1aad9a337fd7a7e45bac037a5338baec4ab2.
> Diff is in https://crrev.com/c/8669854/1..2.
>
> Original change's description:
> > ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions
> >
> > TTC doesn't take an observation after performing an action; page context
> > is delivered separately by TtcPageContextMonitor. Waiting for the page
> > to settle (ObservationDelayController) therefore only delays the tool
> > response to the model, by up to 10s on busy pages.
> >
> > Add ToolRequest::SetSkipObservationDelay(), which makes
> > actor::ToolController complete a tool as soon as it finishes invoking
> > without creating an ObservationDelayController. Follow-up actions
> > enqueued by a tool (e.g. OpenUrlTool's NavigateTool) inherit the flag
> > from the action that enqueued them. TTC sets it on every action it
> > performs.
> >

111960c7d249478fa8b0770693672b035bd138c7 [ diff ] 
Close Media Integrity Providers on navigation

Ensure that media integrity providers cannot outlive the integrity
service impl that is bound to the RenderFrameHost that provided them.
The AwMediaIntegrityServiceImpl gets closed when the main page
navigates. This in turn will now cause all supplied token providers to
also be closed, preventing them from being leaked into a new frame on
navigations.

While the linked bug also calls out that the token providers never
re-validate the origin of the calling frame, doing so is not possible,
since the request for token carries no information about the source
frame.

[Linux] Guard BackgroundModeManager portal integration with USE_DBUS

https://crrev.com/c/8252100 added an unconditional `is_linux` dependency
on `//components/dbus` in
`//chrome/browser/background/extensions/BUILD.gn`, breaking `gn gen` on
`linux-headless-shell-rel` (`use_dbus = false` via
`//build/args/headless.gn`) with `assert(use_dbus)` in
`//dbus/BUILD.gn`.

Guard the `//components/dbus` dependencies in
`//chrome/browser/background/extensions/BUILD.gn` behind `use_dbus`, and
guard the `BackgroundPortal` integration and unit tests in
`BackgroundModeManager` with `BUILDFLAG(USE_DBUS)` so Linux builds with
`use_dbus = false` both generate and compile cleanly.

[TabUI] Remove dead checks from tab list layout delegates

These checks can never trigger. getInsertionIndexOfTab and
NestedLayoutDelegate#ensureGroupHeaderExists take non-null Tab and Token
params under @NullMarked, and every caller already has a non-null value,
so their null guards never run. In NestedLayoutDelegate#onTabAdded the
isTabInTabGroup call repeats the groupId != null check right before it.
An INVALID_TAB_INDEX check in the reorder accessibility actions is
already covered by isValidIndex.

FlatLayoutDelegate#didChangeTabGroupColor was an empty override of an
empty TabGroupObserver default. The didChangeTabGroupTitle override
stays because it blocks a real title update in TabListLayoutDelegate.

[iOS] [Autofill] Re-enable flaky credit card settings EarlGrey tests

Re-enables three tests in `AutofillCreditCardSettingsTestCase`
(`autofill_credit_card_settings_egtest.mm`) that were disabled as
flaky:
- `testCreditCardViewPageMandatoryReauthDisabled` (disabled in
  https://crrev.com/c/6253937)
- `testAccessibilityOnCreditCardViewPage` (disabled in
  https://crrev.com/c/5904498)
- `testAccessibilityOnCreditCardEditPage` (disabled in
  https://crrev.com/c/6904621)

Every test in `autofill_credit_card_settings_egtest.mm` opens the
Payments settings page through `-openCreditCardsSettings`, which uses
`[ChromeEarlGreyUI tapSettingsMenuButton:]` (where
`ScopedDisableTimerTracking` disables EarlGrey `NSTimer` tracking) to
push "Your saved info" (`kSettingsAutofillAndPasswordsCellId`) and then
"Payments" (`PaymentMethodsButton`) onto the settings navigation
controller. On slow simulator bots (`ios-simulator-full-configs`),
`grey_tap` on `PaymentMethodsButton` or on the credit card row was
dispatched while the preceding push transition was still in flight
(`grey_sufficientlyVisible` and `grey_interactable` are already
satisfied while the incoming table view slides in), causing
`UINavigationController` to defer or drop the next push until EarlGrey
timed out with the previous table view still in the hierarchy.
Similarly, pushing the card details page
(`AutofillCreditCardEditTableView`), toggling edit mode
(`NavigationBarEditButton` / `NavigationBarCancelButton`), and popping
back via `SettingsMenuBackButton` raced against
`verifyAccessibilityForCurrentScreen` and `-exitSettingsMenu`.

This CL resolves these navigation transition races by:
- Waiting in `-openCreditCardsSettings` for each outgoing table view
  or row (`kSettingsTableViewId` and `PaymentMethodsButton`) to
  leave the view hierarchy and for `AutofillCreditCardTableView` to
  become sufficiently visible before returning.
- Adding `-waitForCreditCardDetailsPageToAppear` to wait for
  `AutofillCreditCardTableView` to disappear and for
  `AutofillCreditCardEditTableView` and its first field cell
  (`kExpectedFields[0]`) to appear before asserting on fields or
  running `verifyAccessibilityForCurrentScreen`.
- Waiting for `NavigationBarCancelButton` after entering edit mode
  and `NavigationBarEditButton` after leaving edit mode in
  `testAccessibilityOnCreditCardEditPage`.
- Waiting for `AutofillCreditCardTableView` to appear after tapping
  `SettingsMenuBackButton(0)` on the card details page, and waiting in
  `-exitSettingsMenu` for `AutofillCreditCardTableView` to disappear
  and `SettingsDoneButton` to be sufficiently visible before closing
  settings.

All three re-enabled tests were executed and verified passing on
physical `iPhone 13` (iOS 27.0.1) and physical `iPad (A16)`
(iOS 26.7.2), as well as on `iPhone 16 Pro` (iOS 27.0) and `iPad (A16)`
(iOS 27.0) simulators.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[gardener] Attempt to deflake service-worker-wasm.js

After a recent change to V8/WASM [1], this test can fail if a GC
takes place between the instantiateModule() calls. The hypothesis [2]
is that this worked before due to a refcount<->GC retention cycle,
which has now been eliminated (see [2]).

It's a bit unclear what this test tries to actually cover:
It expects `CacheHit` events (from non-persistent cache hits)
in its -expectation.txt, but code/comments seem to expect
to hit a persistent/disk cache. I'd expect that the intent here indeed
was to test the *persistent* cache, but that presumably requires
execution under the appropriate VirtualTestsuite
(virtual/wasm-code-caching).

For now, focus this test around the memory cache, and most importantly:
Retain the result of compileStreaming calls to avoid weak pointers
in the WASM (memory) cache getting cleared.

[1] https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/v8/v8/+/8679040
[2] https://docs.google.com/document/d/1vc_M89tnxFYIQVWKP82_TNDfFiuFXi8nEfxli74r1ns

[a11y] Skip nested child updates while iterating children

ClearChildren(), RemoveSubtreeInternal() and RemoveIncludedSubtree()
walk an object's children_ and call into code that can update the
tree. That code can come back to the same object and clear or rebuild
its children_ while the outer loop is still walking it, for example
through aria-owns processing or image map updates.

This CL adds AXObject::GuardedCachedChildrenIncludingIgnored(), which
those loops use in place of CachedChildrenIncludingIgnored(). Until the
loop ends, nested calls on that object return early:
- UpdateChildrenIfNecessary() leaves the children dirty, so a later
  call rebuilds them.
- ClearChildren() skips clearing, because the outer loop is already
  detaching or removing those children.

A nested call can still detach the object, so each caller checks
IsDetached() after its loop before using the object again.

Outside of these nested calls the behavior is unchanged, because the
guard only lives as long as those loops and only affects the object
whose children are being walked.

[TabUI] Align and simplify TabListDataProvider implementations

Standardizes method ordering, visibility boundaries, and helper checks
across TabListDataProvider, FlatTabListDataProvider, and
GroupedTabListDataProvider so the three providers follow the same
layout.

* Group methods consistently across all three providers (public
  lifecycle -> protected hooks and item operations -> private helpers,
  with TabGroupObserver callbacks in interface order).
* Remove redundant index/filter checks in syncTabItem, moveItemsAt,
  removeTabItem, moveTabItem, removeGroupItem, updateGroupItemAt, and
  indexOfItemForTab.
* When a tab already visible in FlatTabListDataProvider merges into an
  earlier group, TabModel sets its group ID before firing didMoveTab, so
  didMergeTabToGroup (via syncTabItem) is what repositions the item.

[Android] Re-enable CompositorAnimatorTest playSequentially

AnimatorSet starts the next animation from a Choreographer frame, but
nothing ran the main looper after the first animation ended.

idle() never returns while an AnimatorSet runs, because another frame
is always due. Run frames one at a time instead, up to 10. AnimatorSet
needs at most 2 today.

Passes 570 runs on SDK 29 to 37.

24846782a06aaf44ebf67815613d6c5342e5cfde [ diff ] 
[composebox] Make vertical matches container scrollable in dropdown

When multiple two-line text suggestions are shown in the NTP composebox,
the vertical suggestion list can exceed the card's max height. Because
.matches.vertical was not scrollable, arrowing down through suggestions
scrolled the outer #inputContainer instead, pushing the text input and
clear button out of view.

This CL makes the vertical matches list scroll within the dropdown:
- Move min-height: 0, overflow-x/y, and custom scrollbar styles from
  .matches.grid to .matches so both vertical and grid suggestion lists
  scroll independently while keeping the input controls pinned.
- Add tests in composebox_dropdown_test.ts verifying that two-line
  vertical matches scroll without scrolling the parent container, and
  that a group header stays pinned while they scroll.

Screencast: http://screencast/cast/NTYzMDU3MTE3NzgzNjU0NHw1ZjUwYjkyOS0xMw

Bug: b:578993147

4f66b6ddf944cff07a2282dd5fa4c064d389966e [ diff ] 
[Settings] Batch SettingsActivityTest

SettingsActivityTest was annotated with @DoNotBatch because its tests
launch a Settings activity. In addition, testUnfoldKeepsSettingsActivity
had a method-level @EnableFeatures(SETTINGS_IN_TAB) annotation that
would split it into a separate sub-batch, and called
ApplicationTestUtils.recreateActivity() directly instead of
mSettingsActivityTestRule.recreateActivity(), leaving the rule pointing
at the old destroyed activity during cleanup.

This CL switches SettingsActivityTest to @Batch(PER_CLASS), replaces
mSettingsActivityTestRule.getActivity().finish() in tearDown() with
mSettingsActivityTestRule.finishActivity() so each activity reaches
DESTROYED before the next test begins, finishes the extra activities
launched in testStandaloneFragments, and updates
testUnfoldKeepsSettingsActivity to use FeatureOverrides.enable() and
mSettingsActivityTestRule.recreateActivity().

On CQ trybots, this reduces SettingsActivityTest runtime for passing
tests on android-x64-rel from 17.2s to 10.8s (-6.39s / -37%, 1.59x
faster) in chrome_public_test_apk and from 19.5s to 14.6s (-4.89s /
-25%, 1.34x faster) in chrome_public_test_apk_tablet, and on android-
internal-desktop-x64-rel (chrome_public_test_apk) from 29.5s to 14.3s
(-15.2s / -51%, 2.06x faster).

Bug: 578114288
Test: run_chrome_public_test_apk -f "*SettingsActivityTest*" --repeat=19

Fix OnboardingVariantProperties corner radius check without compositing

DialogDelegate::GetCornerRadius() returns 0 when
views::Widget::IsWindowCompositingSupported() is false, which causes
ContextualTasksOnboardingBubbleViewTest.OnboardingVariantProperties to
fail on Linux builders running under Xvfb without a compositing window
manager (such as chrome/ci/linux64).

Check delegate->corner_radius() for the configured value (8) and gate
the expected value of delegate->GetCornerRadius() on
views::Widget::IsWindowCompositingSupported().

TAG=agy
CONV=48246492-dd38-45bf-b570-990f22a727c5

[FragmentedOofInCb] Resume fragmented OOFs in table section.

If a table section is the containing block of out-of-flow positioned
descendants, out-of-flow break tokens may now found directly under the
section.

Update BlockChildIterator to skip child tracking for resumed OOFs. Child
tracking is only used by table section and table row layout, and
sections/rows cannot have direct OOF children.

The test coverage for this was non-existent, so add a test that would
have failed without this fix.

a0e31d794e18340004b192fc12a18bb47f3f693d [ diff ] 
Add SchemeHostPort equality in url/origin.rs

Adds a PartialEq/Eq implementation for url::SchemeHostPort in
url/origin.rs, which defers to the C++ SchemeHostPort::operator== via a
new SchemeHostPortEquals() shim, since CXX does not currently support
calling C++ operators directly.

This will be used by the Rust implementation of
ChildProcessSecurityPolicy to compare the precursor of an opaque
committed origin with the SchemeHostPort of a requested URL [1],
matching ProcessState::MatchesCommittedOrigin() in C++ [2].

[1] https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8681811/1/content/browser/security/cpsp/process_state.rs#623
[2] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/b08aa588efe6119dddce69ec6c9087f31b3b0f5c:content/browser/security/cpsp/child_process_security_policy_impl.cc;l=669-671

tree
5ef1aa7eadd0f452f8cc0b4cdf8591e5c6a870eb
parent
e73ccf0c8ccde835ffb868367fec8ce757b32395 [ diff ] 
android: Update comment placement in input_hint_checker

Yesterday ckitagawa@ found the problem (see below) slightly earlier
than myself  and landed the optimization in crrev.com/c/8680065. Here let
me update the comment placement, as it should have moved with the code
block.

Longer context, but not as long as LLM:

When looking at ANR samples (in crbug.com/574314694) I noticed that many
of them are sitting in NewLocalRef, like so:

  _JNIEnv::NewLocalRef
  jni_zero::ScopedJavaGlobalWeakRef::get
  base::android::InputHintChecker::HasInputImplWithThrottling

This is part of early return code, determining whether there is a View
attached to the input hint checker. It is later followed by the
TimeTicks-based early return.

The calls to AttachCurrentThread(), view_.get(), ~ScopedJavaLocalRef()
are creating/deleting local refs (incl. reference table alloc), checking
global weak reference lock (since view_ is a weakref), making a few
Native-Java-Native transitions, potentially getting suspended during GC.

Bug: 579112189

Quota: add PostTaskWithQuotaManager to QuotaManagerProxy

Inspired by base::SequenceBound's PostTaskWithThisObject, this allows
chaining multiple calls into QuotaManagerImpl without bouncing back to
the calling sequence.

Use this to remove thread trampolines in AwQuotaManagerBridge and
BrowsingDataQuotaHelperImpl.

9348be9c69bf4b2ee9d49750092574ae5a690e7d
parent
02b4730440a4fb79b9ffec91a9b7f83920f15440 [ diff ] 
Mark Window.matchMedia with Affects=Nothing

- Context: DevTools Console eager evaluation and the DevTools AI
  executeJavaScript tool evaluate expressions with throwOnSideEffect:
  true. Because window.matchMedia() lacked [Affects=Nothing], calling it
  aborted with EvalError: Possible side-effect in debug-evaluate.
- Why this was missed: Blink IDL attributes default to
  kHasNoSideEffect, whereas IDL methods default to kHasSideEffect unless
  explicitly annotated with [Affects=Nothing]. window.matchMedia() only
  parses the media query string and returns a new MediaQueryList object
  without mutating DOM or JS state.
- Prior art: window.getComputedStyle() and window.getSelection() in
  window.idl are already marked [Affects=Nothing].

[IA][BottomBar] Turn on education by default

All AndroidBottomBar params already default to the launch candidate
(Arm 1) except education_enabled, so tests and the chrome://flags
"Enabled" option ran the bar without education.

Configs that turn the bar on without this param now get education too,
like the Glic 1A Finch config and the 1A/1B flags. Nothing new shows
for them, since the education bubble and tooltips need IPH features
that are off by default, and the opt-in dialog only applies when the
bar is off by default.

[test] Don't hang ScopedPageFocusOverride after a forced detach
📋 Metadata

author

tree
7248300d34515613806489c2ddef39a09730128c
parent
08c13d3d127f5533dfe2edc121867f6e9a417682 [ diff ] 
[test] Don't hang ScopedPageFocusOverride after a forced detach

~ScopedPageFocusOverride() disables focus emulation and waits for the
DevTools response. If its session was force-detached before that, e.g.
by DevToolsAgentHost::DetachAllClients(), the command is dropped, and
the destructor blocks until the RunLoop timeout fails the test, or the
test launcher kills it.

That's why OmniboxPopupFullTest fails on JS code coverage builders,
e.g. linux-js-coverage-rel, since https://crrev.com/c/8675790. With
--devtools-code-coverage, WebUIMochaBrowserTest collects coverage after
the Mocha run, and DevToolsAgentCoverageObserver::CollectCoverage()
detaches all DevTools clients when done, before the fixture's
TearDownOnMainThread() destroys the override.

Record when the agent host closes the session, stop waiting for a
response then, and skip the cleanup in the destructor. Detaching the
session already ended focus emulation in the browser and the renderer.

Bug: 579145745
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=OmniboxPopupFullTest.* --devtools-code-coverage=/tmp/cov

8e40ebd82fb1695b8bbbb6e440a93cbcfee29dd1 [ diff ] 
Android: Do not take screenshots for skipped tests

AssumptionViolatedException is thrown when a test's preconditions
are not met. The test is then reported as skipped, not as failed.

ScreenshotOnFailureStatement catches every Throwable, so a failure
screenshot is also taken for a skipped test. This can have the side
effect of changing the setup of the accessibility tests that run
after the skipped one.

Rethrow AssumptionViolatedException without taking a screenshot.

[aura] Clear input state on untargeted releases and modal dialogs
📋 Metadata

dd14fff67368846d9081928dbe15a7a59bccf051

tree
f8cc56f76845f4a247336e556e7942ec8a9665ae
parent
74c9c86cbe7bf9b806c4bf7bbcc11b1b33bcfe94 [ diff ] 
[aura] Clear input state on untargeted releases and modal dialogs

aura's touch and mouse button state, drag press records, gesture touch
locks, and pressed mouse handler are normally updated from
WindowEventDispatcher::PreDispatchEvent(). An event that finds no
target never gets there, e.g. while DesktopWindowTreeHostLinux installs
a NullWindowTargeter on the root window during a modal file dialog. A
touch release, touch cancel, or mouse button release in that state was
dropped, leaving the touch or button reported as held, its press
available to start a drag, and the previous target locked to subsequent
input.

Add ui::EventProcessor::OnEventTargetNotFound(), invoked when no target
is found for an event, and have WindowEventDispatcher update
EnvInputStateController for untargeted touch releases, touch cancels,
and mouse button releases, and clear mouse_pressed_handler_ for mouse
button releases. For a touch locked to a window under the dispatcher's
root window, cancel that window's active touches, so it sees an end for
each touch and its gesture; the synthesized cancels are dispatched to
that window directly when they find no target either. Events that are
dispatched are unaffected.

In addition, a modal dialog opened via
DesktopWindowTreeHostLinux::DisableEventListening() may consume an
in-progress touch or mouse release itself so the release never reaches
the host window. Clear tracked mouse button and press state, release
mouse grab tracking, and cancel active touches on the host window when
disabling event listening.

Fixed: 578798220

[UI] Migrate c/b/u/ (part 5) to `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition`

Spell the type as `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition` instead of the
deprecated `WindowOpenDisposition` alias, and include
"ui/base/mojom/window_open_disposition.mojom-shared.h" instead of
"ui/base/window_open_disposition.h". Remove includes of the old header
that nothing uses.

Headers that only use the type in declarations include the mojom
forward header instead.

This CL covers:
  chrome/browser/ui/android/
  chrome/browser/ui/read_anything/
  chrome/browser/ui/toolbar/
  chrome/browser/ui/tab_contents/
  chrome/browser/ui/passwords/
  chrome/browser/ui/serial/
  chrome/browser/ui/omnibox/ (except alternate_nav_infobar_delegate.h,
    which https://crrev.com/c/8649369 migrates)

No behavior change.

`git cl format`:

4cb29ab8ccd29cc4238431ee64b5b9428498651e

3964270eaa44ef17409f43bb2c79ba2683d37490 [ diff ] 
Migrate //chrome/browser/ui/ash to ApplicationLocaleStorage::GetTag()

ApplicationLocaleStorage::Get() returns the application locale as a
std::string. Migrate the remaining //chrome/browser/ui/ash callers to
GetTag(), which returns a base::i18n::LanguageTag, so that callers carry
the type-safe locale representation and Get() can eventually be removed.

GraduationManagerImpl needs the Google-specific form of the locale, so
add a base::i18n::LanguageTag overload of google_util::GetGoogleLocale()
instead of duplicating its "nb" -> "no" mapping. The std::string
overload stays until its remaining callers are migrated.

This is a mechanical, behavior-preserving change: Get() currently
returns exactly std::string(GetTag().tag_string()).

59059f626279546fb7b03b7a8714b62ed35fe1e9
parent
492c8304b6e74045e28bc9daf858b545363aa413 [ diff ] 
[Glic] Log experimental triggering state to the actor journal

GlicEnabling::GetExperimentalTriggeringState() has grown complex, which
makes it hard to debug why a device reports kUnavailable or kNeedsOptIn.
This logs the state and the reason for it to chrome://actor-internals,
following the pattern used by
GlicActorPolicyChecker::ComputeActOnWebCapability().

- Add ExperimentalTriggeringNotReadyReason, which records why the state
  isn't kReady (kNone if it is), similar to CannotActReason.
- Move the state logic into ComputeExperimentalTriggeringState(), which
  returns the state and reason. The kNeedsOptIn/kReady cases now come
  from GetRequiredExperimentalOptIn().
- last_experimental_triggering_state_ now holds the state and reason.
  MaybeNotifyExperimentalTriggeringStateChanged() logs when either
  changes, but only notifies observers when the state does, so
  reason-only changes don't trigger a Sync DeviceInfo refresh.
- MaybeNotifyExperimentalTriggeringStateChanged() is now also called
  from the constructor, so the initial state is logged.

The getter itself doesn't log, as it's called on every Sync DeviceInfo
refresh. The computed state and observer notifications are unchanged.

Observable production logic is unchanged, and so is covered by existing
tests.

To support actor log testing, the existing TTC ActorJournalRecorder is
moved up into the general actor test tools. This class is the most
ergonomic way to test actor journal entries, and I expect further reuse
in the future.

7febeb2b9e19769a160152c78e6654b7fbdea99a
parent
cc82031042d933c8ebd8c24295a423f67288970e [ diff ] 
Avoid unnecessary allocations with MessagePort

All callers of MessagePort::EntanglePorts pass a
blink::Vector<MessagePortChannel>. Removing the unused
std::vector<MessagePortChannel> overload avoids allocating a temporary
std::vector and copying all MessagePortChannel elements via
base::ToVector on every call.

Add defaulted move constructor and move-assignment operator (and default
the copy-assignment operator) to MessagePortChannel so moving a
MessagePortChannel moves its scoped_refptr<State> instead of copying it.
This necessitates removing a redundant std::move on
ProcessorCreationParams::PortChannel() in AudioWorkletProcessor::Create,
which returns a MessagePortChannel by value and triggered
-Wpessimizing-move now that MessagePortChannel has a move constructor.

Use base::ToVector in MessagePortChannel::ReleaseHandles and
MessagePortChannel::CreateFromHandles (which avoids default-constructing
throwaway MessagePortChannel::State instances before overwriting each
element).

📋 Metadata

cc82031042d933c8ebd8c24295a423f67288970e

committer

Fri Oct 30 17:23:26 2026
tree
d5a100856b35494250aa688f8a1837a826acd6f3
parent
ace8e65be19825dd9a016a7082a04b5e9db78217 [ diff ] 
Fix temporary reset of CertVerifierService CT logs on network service crash

CT Logs were initialized to the compiled-in state in
SystemNetworkContextManager::OnNetworkServiceCreatedInternal for both
the CertVerifierServiceFactory and NetworkService. They would then be
set to the current component updated version by calling
PKIMetadataComponentInstallerService::ReconfigureAfterNetworkRestart.
However, this ReconfigureAfterNetworkRestart is asynchronous and
involves reading the component files off the disk. This means that if
the CertVerifierService already had component updated CL list loaded
it would have a brief period where the lists would get reset to the
compiled-in version before being reset back to the component update
version.

A network service crash does not affect the CertVerifierService which
runs in the browser process, so we don't need to reset its state in a
network service crash handler. Move the CertVerifierService static CT
log list initialization to the SystemNetworkContextManager constructor
instead.

d5087df1ceb570382fc4ea78f78ca67a2e57f89d
parent
39d5a49b8bcaab013869e9f08f181da9ba965fcb [ diff ] 
[Glic] Lazily initialize CoBrowse views and bridges on Android

When restoring background tabs on Android with LoadAllTabsAtStartup
enabled, GlicSidePanelCoordinatorAndroid eagerly allocated
CoBrowseViewsBridge and TabBottomSheetBridge for every tab. Because
CoBrowseViewsBridge::window_android_ starts as null,
CoBrowseViewsBridge::OnTabInserted() treated initial TabModel insertion
as a cross-window reparenting and eagerly inflated
R.layout.tab_bottom_sheet and the resizing placeholder layout twice per
restored tab on the UI thread (costing up to 648 ms across 150 tabs)
even when Glic was never shown.

Keep TabFeatures instantiation unconditional so runtime changes to
profile eligibility remain unaffected, defer bridge creation in
GlicSidePanelCoordinatorAndroid until Show(), guard
CoBrowseViewsBridge::OnTabInserted() on existing views (while observing
guest WebContents lifetime), and defer placeholder layout inflation in
WebViewResizingHelper until reset() / setThinWebView().

5be87a8e2ea48a39701d0ab200e49de3328f8798 [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Send handshake capabilities

Search Web needs to know which client-dependent capabilities Chrome
supports during the rearchitecture Service Worker handshake so it can
hide unsupported extension UI such as the tabs picker in Incognito or
when tab context sharing is disabled. Previously, `GetHandshakeMessage`
serialized a legacy `ClientToAimMessage` with `FeatureCapability` enum
values rather than a `ClientToSearchMessage`.

Add the `Capability` enum (`TABS`, `LENS`) to
`ClientToSearchMessage.HandshakePing` in `search_communication.proto`
and introduce `GetSearchHandshakeMessageProto` to populate `LENS` and
conditionally include `TABS` when `CanShareTabContext` is true.

Check upload provider WeakPtr on its own sequence

c4f79988e58603b4951cdc247282aa9335b55298

tree
33b34b957dc3d0335096ca6507f894ea5b240a18
parent
b191ae4d588bd2f9ab687ec2197610b20e5fa780 [ diff ] 
Check upload provider WeakPtr on its own sequence

ReportingClient hands every Uploader a callback holding a WeakPtr to the
upload provider. Each Uploader runs that callback on its own thread pool
sequence, so when two uploaders overlap the WeakPtr gets checked on two
different sequences and trips a sequence checker DCHECK. This made
WebstorePrivatePolicyTest.ExtensionRequestPolicy (which enables cloud
reporting) crash intermittently in android_browsertests. Even with a
single uploader, using the WeakPtr off the client sequence could race
with ReportingClient destroying the upload provider.

Post the callback back to the ReportingClient sequence, which owns the
upload provider, before touching the WeakPtr. Add a unit test that
deterministically reproduced the crash with overlapping uploaders.

7e04e2c657bee68dc1d4834623265b1d4b77487d
parent
435cedec35d6fb9d9c9dc1eff6ad9a0574f02bec [ diff ] 
[Settings] Defragment MultiColumnSettingsTest batching

MultiColumnSettingsTest was parameterized with @ClassParameter for
MAKE_IDENTITY_MANAGER_SOURCE_OF_ACCOUNTS and mocked SigninManager,
TemplateUrlService, and SyncService in setUp() only because
testSinglePane and testTwoPane launched MainSettings without skipping
preference inflation. In addition, 14 test methods had method-level
@EnableFeatures or @DisableFeatures annotations, splitting the suite
into 7 feature-flag sub-batches per class parameter (up to 14 sub-
batches total).

This CL calls MainSettings.setSkipPreferencesForTesting(true) in
setUp(), removes the redundant @ClassParameter and service mocks,
replaces method-level @EnableFeatures/@DisableFeatures annotations with
in-test FeatureOverrides calls so all 19 tests run in a single batch,
and uses finishActivity() in tearDown().

On CQ trybots, this reduces MultiColumnSettingsTest runtime for passing
tests on android-x64-rel from 14.1s to 5.82s (-8.29s / -59%, 2.42x
faster) in chrome_public_test_apk and from 35.4s to 20.0s (-15.4s /
-43%, 1.77x faster) in chrome_public_test_apk_tablet, and on android-
internal-desktop-x64-rel (chrome_public_test_apk) from 45.7s to 22.3s
(-23.3s / -51%, 2.04x faster).

Bug: 578114288
Test: run_chrome_public_test_apk -f "*MultiColumnSettingsTest*" --repeat=19

parent
ef496ccf3632c0c31a481d1a3fa116e5c59ee618 [ diff ] 
[Android/TabUI] Coalesce updateLayout in TabListMediator batch reset

During cold Hub / Grid Tab Switcher open with N tabs, each tab card
addition triggers ModelList observer notifications that invoke
updateLayout() N times. Each layout update re-queries price welcome card
eligibility and recalculates span size allocations across all items,
costing redundant main thread work and span inspections during batch
population.

Suppress updateLayout() notifications while rebuilding the ModelList in
resetWithListOfTabs and execute a single coalesced updateLayout() pass
once all tabs and groups have been inserted.

Bug: 579505319

tree
551b69937c1ecd1726774fbe5ea75c41062529ef
parent
e944c20454824f24d5a2cbfa07e5451a913564ce [ diff ] 
Cancel in-flight favicon requests when an error page commits

A committed error page replaces the last committed NavigationEntry
without starting a new favicon lookup. Previously this left the
preceding page's favicon requests active, allowing a delayed result to
update the error page's entry or trip the URL-matching DCHECK.

Cancel the pending history, candidate, manifest and image work when an
error page commits. Share that cancellation with FetchFavicon(), while
preserving the existing same-document URL associations.

The regression test holds a tracked favicon response until after the
error commit, verifies that a lookup was issued and cancelled, and
checks that the stale response neither notifies observers nor modifies
the error-page icon. Add coverage for same-document navigation after
candidates were received.

Bug: 549900459
Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter=ContentFaviconDriver*.*:FaviconHandler*.*

tree
ecb1e3f825b1ccfe0a695f6eea1ef7a41cc875c4
parent
cda2a6b1521d6cb9c2bad45989edaf930e6b2dd1 [ diff ] 
[searchbox] Disallow JS execution in SearchBoxExtension::Install

SearchBoxExtension::Install() runs from DidClearWindowObject() during
navigation commit. When a same-origin subframe commits from its initial
empty document, its v8::Context is reused, so page-defined accessors on
Object.prototype or window.chrome can survive into Install().

Guard javascript from running in installation with a
DisallowJavascriptExecutionScope and use CreateDataProperty() instead of
Set() so during install, page-defined accessors are not triggered. Bail
out cleanly instead of CHECK-crashing if any property operation fails.

Bug: 578689876

committer

Fri Oct 30 17:03:41 2026
tree
b2fa35e2a881396dc9764d0275affdd4e88c6ddf
parent
adb9af6236d0f9268bd2f444ab3ad17e55f2f05e [ diff ] 
[line-clamp] Floats shouldn't grow the height of line-clamp containers

When a line-clamp container clamps, floats before the clamp point
don't grow the container past the clamp point. Instead, they get
clipped to the container's block-end content edge. But when the
container doesn't clamp, floats can make the container grow beyond the
point at which it'd clamp by height. This is our current behavior in
Chromium.

As per https://github.com/w3c/csswg-drafts/pull/14569, the CSSWG
resolved that if the line-clamp container doesn't clamp, floats would
also not grow the container, and they would also be clipped at the
block-end content edge. This CL implements this.

tree
8caee737236256b5878999ceeba88be5fa1af431
parent
79efbcb75f2d97affb02a7f51ed7d9a314e3a535 [ diff ] 
[rustmojo] Track & close unregistered interface IDs

When a message contains a mojo handle, and it gets dropped or
rejected or so on, Mojo automatically closes the handle for us and
sends any necessary disconnect notifications. However, with associated
interface IDs, we need to do all that manually.

This CL introduces a new field to the message type which tracks the
associated IDs that the message carries. It then makes sure to close
them under the various circumstances where they get dropped before
being fully parsed into a `PendingAssociated*` object.

This CL also adds yet another parsing output, so it creates a new
`SerializeData` type to represent the return value of that function,
and tweaks the code to use it everywhere.

Originally this CL attempted to use a stronger wrapper with a drop
handler to ensure that the associated interface IDs got cleaned up,
but that proved to be infeasibly complicated, so we end up with the
current version which relies on the multiplex router doing the
cleanup whenever necessary.

Introduce a generic UploadTask pipeline for PrivateInsightsService

Refactors PrivateInsightsService to introduce PendingEventsBatch and
UploadTask abstractions. This allows decoupling upload logic from
specific event proto types and offloads event serialization from the
main sequence to the thread pool.

Adds the kPrivateInsightsUploadTaskPipeline base feature to gate the new
pipeline for Contextual Cues while maintaining the legacy upload path
for rollout safety. Tests are updated and parameterized to cover both
pipelines.

tree
a191b9e6694eeb09ff4df974e00b2fb21f4ca685
parent
d9abee0576a5c8fdf0b4a6824d1b489c65f16950 [ diff ] 
[DeepCAF] Harden loading custom form control scripts

Make CustomFormControlScriptExecutor::LoadScript() stricter about what
counts as a script's manager:

- Shadow the entry point's name, createCustomFormControlManager, in an
  outer function. Otherwise, if a script doesn't declare it, the lookup
  falls through to the isolated world's global object and finds a
  function that an earlier script for the same document assigned to
  window.createCustomFormControlManager. The script runs in a separate
  inner function, so it can still declare the entry point with let or
  const without redeclaring the parameter.
- Don't accept a function, array or promise as the manager; V8 counts
  them as objects too. A promise is what an async entry point returns,
  which would otherwise be loaded successfully and fail on every call.

Also test that a subframe's manager is dropped when the subframe is
detached, which doesn't commit a new document, and that a script that
calls document.open(), which keeps the document's script contexts,
still loads.

Bug: b:578383598
Test: components_browsertests --gtest_filter=CustomFormControlScriptExecutorTest.*

from ContextualTasksLensInteractionBrowserTest, official/branded builds
(where fieldtrial_testing_config.json is disabled and kContextualTasks
defaults to disabled while kContextualTasksSidePanel is enabled) fail in
SubsequentRegionSelectionLoadsNewResult and
SubsequentRegionSelectionFromComposeboxAddsVisualSelection.

TestingContextualTasksUiService::StartTaskUiInSidePanelImpl hosts
stub_web_contents_ in the tab strip for testing rather than the side
panel view. Because IsWebContentsInSidePanel(stub_web_contents_) returns
false, ContextualTasksUiService::HandleNavigationImpl treats the
navigation to chrome://contextual-tasks/ as a standalone tab navigation
and redirects it to AIM when kContextualTasks is disabled.

Override IsWebContentsInSidePanel in TestingContextualTasksUiService to
return true for stub_web_contents_.

f9f8ec5600fcfe2fe48d83b2e5c2c83a838adbb6
parent
f85c6effbd3a98c503ae60e57ee9ceb6c27b820b [ diff ] 
[Actor] Port CloseWindow and ActivateWindow to Android

The window management tool only supported EnterFullscreen and
ExitFullscreen on Android. CloseWindow and ActivateWindow were compiled
out because they used TabStripModel and RegisterDidBecomeActive, neither
of which exists on Android's BrowserWindowInterface.

CloseWindow now iterates the window's tabs through TabListInterface,
which is cross-platform. The BrowserDidClose subscription already works
on Android.

ActivateWindow calls GetWindow()->Activate() on Android, which moves the
window's task to the front. Android's BrowserWindowInterface doesn't
report activation changes and moving a task to the front is asynchronous
in the OS, so the tool completes immediately after making the request
instead of waiting for the window to become active as it does on
desktop.

The three window ToolRequests were guarded as a unit in
ToolRequestVariant, the visitor, the UI event dispatcher, the proto
conversion, and the tool registry tests. This CL un-guards all three so
the plumbing compiles on Android. CreateWindow isn't ported yet, so on
Android its Invoke() returns a kToolUnknown error rather than crashing
if a request reaches it (e.g. from a proto action). TTC does not
dispatch any window tools yet.

The CloseWindow, CloseWindowRemovesTabFromActingSet, and ActivateWindow
browser tests now run on Android. Since the CreateWindow tool isn't
available there, the tests open the second window with
CreateBrowserWindow() and wait for activation by polling the last-active
window.

NO_IFTTT=No variant types added or removed; only Android guards dropped.

[contextual_tasks] Show the searchbox in Incognito; hide explore bar

In the SiC rearchitecture, opening a new thread in the Lens side panel
in Incognito showed no searchbox. ContextualTasksURLLoaderThrottle
skipped off-the-record profiles, so Search did not get the
Chrome-Search-Capabilities-Version header and treated Incognito as a
legacy client. This caused the searchbox to not show, along with the aim
header showing incorrectly.

This lack of header also caused the AIM explore bar / AIM tab to show
when in AIM side panel (which is opened after clicking on "AI mode" in
lens side panel).

The lack of separate mojo connection bridge and shared regular and
incognito profiles also prevents the extension from working properly.

Fix:
- Drops the IsOffTheRecord() check that makes off the record profiles
  not eligible. This ensures that the header exists in incognito and
  thus the searchbox can be rendered.
- Adds allowlist that allows eligibility only for regular and incognito,
  while disallowing others (like guest).
- Runs the extension in split incognito mode, so Incognito frames
  connect to an Incognito instance of its service worker, not shared.
- Gives off-the-record profiles their own ContextualTasksExtensionBridge
  (like ContextualTasksService and ContextualTasksUiService), so that
  instance has its Mojo binders and load time data.

2c2355a4c0e743faa322bd06b35ce79a56dbae9e
parent
4605480adb5688cb3acc22af42873193d6d5e971 [ diff ] 
[line-clamp] Make "relayout without clamping" not disable line-clamp

When line-clamping based on a number of lines, the clamp line must not
be ellipsized if it's the last line in the line-clamp container. The
way this is currently handled is by detecting this case, and doing a
relayout with line-clamp disabled (`LineClampData::State::kDisabled`).

This used to work because, if there is no clamp, the line-clamp
container behaved exactly as if it were a regular independent block
formatting context.

However, float clipping introduces a difference between line-clamp
containers that don't clamp, and regular independent block formatting
contexts. So we need to add a specific state to `LineClampData::State`
for a line-clamp container which will never clamp. Adding this
`kDontClamp` state also allows us to get rid of the
`BlockLineClampData::ignore_line_clamp` flag, which was used to ignore
the `line-clamp` style in this relayout case.

This patch doesn't add any difference in behavior, it only does a
refactor that will then be used to implement the float clipping
behavior. It therefore doesn't make any changes to the tests.

[InfoBars][1/2] Add compile-time gate for ConfirmInfoBar creation

Introduces ConfirmInfoBarCreationAllowlist using base::PassKey to gate
the creation of desktop confirm infobars (CreateConfirmInfoBar,
ConfirmInfoBar::Create, and CreateSimpleAlertInfoBar).

This ensures that new Desktop infobars use the centralized
BrowserInfoBarManager framework rather than directly subclassing
ConfirmInfoBarDelegate.

This CL:
- Defines ConfirmInfoBarCreationAllowlist (in
  confirm_infobar_creation_allowlist.h) with GetPassKeyForTesting() and
  scoped friendship for BrowserInfoBarManager, permanent exceptions, and
  existing unmigrated delegates/callers (burndown list).
- Adds the base::PassKey<ConfirmInfoBarCreationAllowlist> overload to
  CreateConfirmInfoBar, ConfirmInfoBar::Create, and
  CreateSimpleAlertInfoBar.
- Updates BrowserInfoBarManager to use the PassKey API.
- Adds guidance comments above enum InfoBarIdentifier in
  infobar_delegate.h.
- Retains deprecated transitional non-PassKey overloads so existing
  callers continue to compile until migrated in the second CL.

Design doc: go/infobar-gate

parent
05e46e7b6624d73c55d595246915ca0a0386c384 [ diff ] 
webui-toolbar: Fix PerformanceIntervention tests

When the WebUI performance intervention button uses HighlightTracker for
click suppression, Kombucha PressButton calls with default InputType
dispatch synthetic mouse pointerdown and click events without
transferring OS focus away from the native bubble widget. Because the
button remains highlighted during pointerdown, HighlightTracker
suppresses the synthetic click and the bubble never closes. Use
InputType::kKeyboard when pressing the toolbar button in
PerformanceInterventionInteractiveTest so synthetic activation is not
suppressed by HighlightTracker.

Additionally, track active icon state on
PerformanceInterventionButtonElement until the user clicks the toolbar
button itself, rather than tying active icon color solely to
anchor-highlight. In Views, dismissing the dialog via the dialog
dismiss button leaves the toolbar button icon in its active color until
the toolbar button is clicked, which the InterventionToolbarButton pixel
test expects.

parent
33e88a17f63a4d5e90d459cecdc91c2d1b327392 [ diff ] 
[remoting] Sign remoting_network/peer_connection/curtain on Windows

The Windows host multi-process split added three standalone executables:
remoting_network.exe, remoting_peer_connection.exe and
remoting_curtain.exe. They were added to the build targets, the host
archive deps, host_payload_files.gni and chromoting.wxs, but not to the
"sign" list in parameters.json, which the official signing scripts use
to decide which payload binaries get Authenticode-signed.

As a result, official MSIs (e.g. 157.0.8103.7) ship these three binaries
unsigned. On machines that enforce WDAC/AppLocker, the daemon fails to
launch the network process with "An Application Control policy has
blocked this file. (0x11C7)", so the host never comes online.

Add the three binaries to the sign list. All 14 .exe/.dll entries in
remoting_win_host_payload_files are now covered by "sign".

TAG=agy
CONV=3e102818-4386-4e30-9cca-ec5223de8647

a1feab73d7b0b004a56b59295511d8eb6c93e7ea

Fri Oct 30 16:30:20 2026
tree
79b3d156315dca196147ca70785940300279f83c
parent
99b6c46a241a5a489366c8fb3b782bd11fa88f5b [ diff ] 
[inline-layout] Monospace (21/21) Enable by default

This enables compact storage for eligible horizontal monospace shaping
runs, reducing memory by storing glyph IDs with a shared advance and
implicit character indices instead of full per-glyph records. HarfBuzz
still performs shaping. Eligible runs have a 1:1 mapping to UTF-16 code
units, uniform advances, no initial offsets, and safe break boundaries.
Both LTR and RTL runs are supported.

On a sample 8mb plain text file, this reduces overall renderer memory
from 139mb to 106mb, a 24% reduction. There are also overall performance
gains as well, including the following blink_perf.layout results:

chapter-reflow-pre: +18.7%

editing_insert: +6.5%

editing_delete: +6.4%

editing_prepend: 6.2%

editing_append: 5.4%

The Blink runtime feature flag "MonospaceGlyphCompaction" allows for a
Finch kill switch.

Add post-detach TTL retention and capacity eviction to CdpActorBuffer

Extend CdpActorBuffer with:
- Post-detach TTL retention (kCdpAttributionDetachedActorTtl, default
  30s) via ExternalActorEntry::detached_at and PruneExpiredEntries()
  to handle asynchronous event reporting after an automation session
  disconnects.
- Bounded circular buffer capacity (kCdpAttributionMaxActorsPerTab,
  default 5) via EvictOneEntryForInsert(), evicting the oldest detached
  entry before the oldest attached entry.

[Prefs] Serialize pending lossy writes on a background sequence
📋 Metadata
commit
2ea3d1e0f21668bf93e7e21b917eef7d04404754
[ log ]
author
Calder Kitagawa <ckitagawa@chromium.org>
Fri Oct 30 16:26:16 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 30 16:26:16 2026
tree
8a81bfe0007bdb2dbbf14c16ce713c750ded8798
parent
a7eb35e5310292a2290bfe562c6045fb2bb06299 [ diff ] 
[Prefs] Serialize pending lossy writes on a background sequence

Normal preference writes in JsonPrefStore already serialize JSON on a
background task runner, but flushing pending lossy writes (such as on
Android activity pause or browser shutdown) still used the foreground
serializer path. This blocked the UI thread while serializing the entire
preference dictionary to JSON and downgraded any already-pending normal
write back to foreground serialization.

Switch pending lossy writes to use the background serializer so all
preference JSON serialization happens off the main sequence, and remove
the now-unused foreground serializer implementation. Also skip
scheduling if the store is read-only.

Fixed: 579107284

Skip Skia Gold failures on android-canary-x64-fyi-rel

supported render test config, so Gold mismatches fail render tests on
this FYI builder. Baselines are only maintained for CQ configs, so pass
--skia-gold-consider-unsupported to the instrumentation suites, like
other non-CQ Android builders do.

[tvOS] Filter out TransformToRootCoordinatesClampsFoo browser tests

Relaxing the IsCrossProcessSubframe() assertion would not make
RendererDataValidationNestedFramesBrowserTest meaningful on tvOS.
TransformToRootCoordinates() skips frames whose RenderWidgetHostView is
the same as the one it just clamped against, and frames that share a
local root share a single view, so the clamping these tests cover only
runs with out-of-process iframes.

[media] Disable D3D11 shared handle decoder on AMD
📋 Metadata
commit
5229121256d0e987311e19b99ef3ef3710c30a7d
[ log ]
author
Kramer Ge <fangzhoug@chromium.org>
Fri Oct 30 16:22:33 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 30 16:22:33 2026
tree
f37135802df502dbefbb3202ee50f6e56e7479b5
parent
afe7b0e4e2e6419e28710c3b13551e9cbb831f12 [ diff ] 
[media] Disable D3D11 shared handle decoder on AMD

Enabling D3D11VideoDecoderUseSharedHandle causes single-texture D3D11
video decoders to allocate NV12 output textures with
D3D11_RESOURCE_MISC_SHARED_NTHANDLE | D3D11_RESOURCE_MISC_SHARED. On
Windows with modern AMD GPUs (where VP9 and AV1 use single-texture
decoding), allocating D3D11_BIND_DECODER textures with
D3D11_RESOURCE_MISC_SHARED triggers a driver bug that corrupts the top
rows of decoded video frames with green and purple glare.

Add the disable_d3d11_video_decoder_shared_handle GPU driver bug
workaround for Windows AMD GPUs (0x1002), gate use_shared_handle_ on it
in D3DVideoDecoder, and re-enable D3D11VideoDecoderUseSharedHandle by
default on non-AMD GPUs.

Bug: 402171652, 40270305
Fixed: 577963606
Test: pixel_skia_gold_passthrough_test on gpu-fyi-try-win11-amd-rel-64

aad08cc6956b9d22b53819b3be04d66cac51760b
parent
57aba6e8caff07e9cc9df1e7af95c8850bcac8cf [ diff ] 
signin] Propagate errors from OnOAuthMultiloginFinished to PO2TS

chrome://signin-internals displays token binding failures exactly the
same as LST invalidation errors. https://crrev.com/c/8564199 added
separate InvalidGaiaCredentialsReason values to distinguish between the
two for the access token fetch flow.

This change propagates the GoogleServiceAuthError from
OAuthMultiloginHelper::OnOAuthMultiloginFinished to
ProfileOAuth2TokenService::InvalidateTokenForMultilogin so that bound
refresh token invalidations in the OAML flow record the specific token
binding failure reason. This is not expected to cause any user-visible
behavior changes beyond showing the specific token binding error in
chrome://signin-internals.

[Unified Tabstrip] Add trailing padding for collapsed group header

When a collapsed tab group is the last item in the horizontal tab strip,
its header chip ends flush with the container edge, leaving 0px padding
before the New Tab Button (whereas an expanded group ends with a tab
whose bottom extension provides trailing space).

Add trailing padding (GetTrailingGroupHeaderPadding()) before the New
Tab Button when the last visible child in
UnpinnedTabContainerViewHorizontalLayout is a collapsed TabGroupView,
and account for the trailing vs. non-trailing spacing delta when locking
tab widths on mouse/touch group collapse.

fc4cc82763ae0c61d9a08ab0eb6e51df71c7e56a
parent
3bf4d0891d43b8f9dff1d5ef591d71851d322a55 [ diff ] 
[gap-decorations] Move multicol code to subclass

GapGeometry holds the intersection logic for every container type in one
class. Each container's code is being moved into its own GapGeometry
subclass, so that each container's code is in its own file and
container-specific methods are only available on the matching type.

This CL moves GenerateCrossIntersectionListForMulticol into a new
multicol_gap_geometry.cc. The base class switch in
GenerateCrossIntersectionList now calls it through
To<MulticolGapGeometry>. The method is private, and GapGeometry is a
friend until a later CL changes the dispatch to virtual methods.

Multicol has no fields of its own to move, because spanner main gaps are
already part of the base class.

There is no change in behavior.

[TabUI] Merge dropped GTS cards by tab list instead of tab ID
📋 Metadata

1d98e1a8940264a421ba3dbeaa411df0d14f48e3
[ log ]
author
Gazal <agazal@google.com>
Fri Oct 30 16:14:56 2026
committer

Fri Oct 30 16:14:56 2026
tree
05a4b78b2fa3fef4749aa432ed70ead37ce08370
parent
0c0b65a871a36337de618ef880069068a97a9aa5 [ diff ] 
[TabUI] Merge dropped GTS cards by tab list instead of tab ID

Drop-to-merge in the tab switcher grid passed two tab IDs to
TabModel#mergeTabsToGroup, which looked both tabs up again and
re-collected the dragged card's group. It also asked
TabModel#willMergingCreateNewGroup whether the drop would create a
group. The callback already holds the tabs of both cards, so it can pass
them straight to TabModel#mergeListOfTabsToGroup.

In the grouped grid every card is either a standalone tab or a whole
group, so a drop creates a new group only when both cards are standalone
tabs. That check needs no TabModel query, and this was the last caller
of willMergingCreateNewGroup, so it is deleted from TabModel and its
implementations.

Preserve last animated PaintWorklet value across commit teardown
📋 Metadata
commit
ab7b4f33bb1b2399447befc02223b2aa174636e3
[ log ]
author
Kevin Ellis <kevers@google.com>
Fri Oct 30 16:13:14 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 30 16:13:14 2026
tree
098bfb2fac52e4e882319152c1cce9c3f17fe45c
parent
3f530b03fd383dc2767ed5064934e3aeee04aed7 [ diff ] 
Preserve last animated PaintWorklet value across commit teardown

When a composited PaintWorklet animation finishes on the impl thread,
ClientLayerTreeHostImpl::FinishCommit resets animation_value via
AnimatedPaintWorkletTracker::ClearUnusedInputProperties before ticking
animations on the pending tree. If the animation has completed and no
longer mutates the property during AnimatePendingTreeAfterCommit,
animation_value remains unset even when the committed tree still
carries a dirty PaintWorkletInput for that property (for example,
when another property on the same element triggered a main-thread
repaint while the background-color animation was composited).

Previously, GetPropertyAnimationValue returned an empty PropertyValue
in this state, causing the PaintWorklet to fall back to the main
thread seed value, potentially from the start of the animation,
and flicker for a frame.

Update AnimatedPaintWorkletTracker::GetPropertyAnimationValue to fall
back to last_animation_value when animation_value is unset. The main
thread value is still used to seed the worklet before the first
compositor tick, while preserving the final animated value until main
stops emitting a PaintWorkletInput for the property.

tree
8412afe980431848ab9d5a568e398a244ecfe76f
parent
5ce6d07913e776f2f5663bab5aaf5af7786e15c6 [ diff ] 
Remove WTF::Threading class

WTF::Threading was a leftover wrapper from WebKit containing only three fields:
- thread_id_: never read anywhere in the codebase.
- cached_converter_icu_: only used in text_codec_icu.cc (moved there as a file-local thread-specific static).
- thread_stack_size_: only used on Windows MSVC in stack_util.cc (moved there as a file-local thread_local).

This change removes the Threading class, WtfThreading(), and Threading::Initialize(), leaving blink::CurrentThread() untouched.

f75f0acb694b03d149684c159db0d7564ca0bbb2
parent
800ede50ddcfc8ab52fde479aecb6b0c389340e5 [ diff ] 
[Bedrock] Convert c/t:{test_support,test_support_ui} to group

Both targets have sources = [] and act purely as public_deps aggregators.
group() is the correct GN primitive for this role and avoids the need
for the workaround comments explaining why source_set was used instead
of static_library (empty archive rejected by the Windows librarian).

Changes:
  - source_set("test_support") -> group("test_support")
  - source_set("test_support_ui") -> group("test_support_ui")
  - Remove sources = [] from both targets
  - Remove data = [] from test_support (data_deps retained)
  - Update comments on both targets
  - Move 'runtimeobject.lib' windows library dep to
    c/b/webshare:test_support, which it actually belongs to

Analysis/Plan: https://notes.nickdiego.dev/chromium/bedrocking-chrome-test

[Bedrock] Remove dead deps from chrome/test:test_support

With sources = [], all non-public deps in chrome/test:test_support
served only the sources that have since been extracted to dedicated
targets. They no longer compile anything and contribute nothing to
consumers (only public_deps propagate). Remove them:

  - //chrome/browser/profiles/batch_upload:test_support (enable_dice_support||is_chromeos)
  - //chrome/browser/ui/web_applications/test:test_support (enable_extensions, already in public_deps)
  - //chrome/browser/badging:test_support (!is_android)
  - //chrome/browser/lookalikes:test_support (!is_android)
  - //chrome/browser/webshare:test_support (is_win)
  - //ash/constants, //chrome/browser/ash/printing:test_support,
    //components/component_updater/ash:test_support,
    //google_apis:test_support, //net:test_support (is_chromeos)
  - //components/omnibox/browser:test_support (toolkit_views)
  - //chrome/browser/compose:test_support (enable_compose)

Also remove the now-empty deps = [] initializer.

GN check passes on linux, cros, and android.

Analysis/Plan: https://notes.nickdiego.dev/chromium/bedrocking-chrome-test

[ReadAloud][UMA] Add observability metrics for Standard Playback and speech synthesis

Adds UMA metrics for native Standard Playback startup and speech
synthesis, mirroring the observability pattern from Overview mode.

Metrics added:
- ReadAloud.StandardPlayback.Outcome (startup success/failure)
- ReadAloud.StandardPlayback.TimeToFirstAudio (perceived latency)
- ReadAloud.Synthesis.ModelExecution.ServerResult (RPC result per chunk)
- ReadAloud.Synthesis.ModelExecution.AbortReason (pre-request aborts)
- ReadAloud.Synthesis.ModelExecution.Error (model execution error codes)

Note: Remote model execution round-trip latency is already measured by
OptimizationGuide.ModelExecution.FetchLatency2.ReadAloudGenerateText.

Remove Protected Audience Web platform bindings and features

Following the deprecation of the Protected Audience API and disabling of
Fledge and AdInterestGroupAPI by default (https://crrev.com/c/8451762),
remove the remaining stubbed WebIDL bindings, runtime-enabled features,
and deprecation definitions:

- Delete third_party/blink/renderer/modules/ad_auction/
  (NavigatorAuction, ProtectedAudience, Ads, and associated
  dictionaries), the unused Parakeet Mojom definitions in
  third_party/blink/public/mojom/parakeet/, and the obsolete
  'turtledove' entry in WATCHLISTS.
- Remove the adAuctionHeaders attribute from HTMLIFrameElement and
  RequestInit, along with ProtectedAudience from the stable web-exposed
  surface.
- Remove the Fledge, AdInterestGroupAPI, Parakeet,
  EnforceAnonymityExposure, and Fledge* runtime-enabled features, their
  WebView and fieldtrial testing config overrides, and the Fledge and
  InterestGroupDailyUpdateUrl deprecation entries.

9767ccd8bf4a2d28176065c3c63502e20dea2e77
[ log ]
author

tree
57aa0e87d70d0d783359c6db9582d7799fef03b4
parent
885184881908c95e4557803916dcfa9efb3a9b06 [ diff ] 
Add raw_ptr dep to the Skia Rust BMP and JPEG codec targets

Skia is moving its internal pointer fields to raw_ptr. The Rust BMP
and JPEG codec targets compile Skia internals outside of //skia, and
their sources now pull in src/codec/SkSwizzler.h, which includes
partition_alloc/pointers/raw_ptr.h. //skia keeps that dependency
private, so the build fails to find the header.

The skia_source_set targets already depend on raw_ptr for this reason.
Do the same for rust_bmp_ffi_cpp and rust_jpeg_ffi_cpp.

Bug: 351867706
Doc: https://bit.ly/miracleptr-skia

cf59ca8a779384fdcc471e6cd344fcf64e333552 [ diff ] 
[OTP] Stop Gmail retrieval and clear cache on opt-out

This is a part of OtpManager refactoring preparation.

Previously, OnGmailOtpFillingEnabledPrefChanged() only acted when the
pref became enabled, and MaybeShowOtpSuggestionsForGmail() cached and
delivered tokens after the async PhishGuard check without re-verifying
consent.

This CL stops any in-flight GmailOtpRetriever and clear cached Gmail OTP
state immediately when the user opts out, and re-checks consent after
PhishGuard completes.

Migrate PageContentCache tab IDs on Desktop session restore
📋 Metadata

[ log ]
author

Fri Oct 30 15:46:21 2026
tree
d1a3ad2573b20c544ebf386cb0346db9877e92c2
parent
80081628df24eab85568467f96dd7ca916fbef52 [ diff ] 
Migrate PageContentCache tab IDs on Desktop session restore

On Desktop, session restore creates a new SessionTabHelper with a fresh
SessionID::NewUnique() for each restored tab, even though SessionRestore
deserializes the saved tab_id from disk. Previously, this meant
PageContentCache entries keyed by SessionTabHelper::IdForTab could no
longer be looked up for restored tabs across browser restarts.

This change adds UpdateTabId(old_tab_id, new_tab_id) across
PageContentExtractionService, PageContentCache, and PageContentStore,
and calls it from SessionRestoreImpl::RestoreTab on local session
restores to migrate cached page content to the restored tab's new
SessionID.

Bug: b:550510181

[icu] Check std::isfinite in TimeZone::GetOffset/InDaylightTime
📋 Metadata

Fri Oct 30 15:39:10 2026
tree
1256e573746e58f3cb0402010ad4089720e08c8e
parent
223447144b9d9aacf4e83b1408c81e1f3d5cffe4 [ diff ] 
[icu] Check std::isfinite in TimeZone::GetOffset/InDaylightTime

Since https://crrev.com/c/8507066, `TimeZone::GetOffset()` and
`TimeZone::InDaylightTime()` return early when `time.is_inf()` to avoid
passing a non-finite timestamp to ICU (which fails `DCHECK(U_SUCCESS)`).
However, `time.InMillisecondsFSinceUnixEpoch()` computes
`time - UnixEpoch()`, which underflows and returns `-infinity` for
finite `Time` values near `Time::Min()` (e.g. `Time::Min() + Days(1)`),
so a non-finite timestamp is still passed to ICU.

This CL checks `!std::isfinite()` on the result of
`time.InMillisecondsFSinceUnixEpoch()` instead of `time.is_inf()`.

781e3c8ee33e2566fe561948c4128b2105ad690d [ diff ] 
[PageImageService] Run callback when Suggest request cannot be started

If the TemplateURL matched for a Google SRP has no valid suggestions URL
(for example, when a starter pack engine such as @aimode is returned by
GetTemplateURLForHost("www.google.com")),
RemoteSuggestionsService::StartSuggestionsRequest() returns nullptr.

Previously, SuggestEntityImageURLFetcher::Start() dropped `callback_` in
that case, violating ImageService::FetchImageFor()'s contract that the
callback is always invoked, leaking the fetcher/callback cycle, and
preventing Android bookmarks from falling back to the page favicon.

When `loader_` is null, record PageImageServiceResult::kResponseMissing
and post `callback_` with an empty GURL.

Handle reentrant destruction from clients in `mojo::DataPipeDrainer`
📋 Metadata

781e3c8ee33e2566fe561948c4128b2105ad690d
[ log ]
author
Daniel Cheng <dcheng@chromium.org>
Fri Oct 30 15:34:11 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 30 15:34:11 2026
tree
d791e58d7330b96c0b6eca3867dd439005546cd6
parent
2cfbba13cf3a60f4d915dbef2ea5d16949e42c0f [ diff ] 
Handle reentrant destruction from clients in `mojo::DataPipeDrainer`

`DataPipeDrainer::ReadData()` called `source_->EndReadData()`
unconditionally after `client_->OnDataAvailable()`; however, a client
that reentrantly destroyed the `mojo::DataPipeDrainer` would trigger a
use-after-free. Avoiding this use-after-free requires additional
handling (https://crrev.com/c/2672379, https://crrev.com/c/7781209);
forgetting to handle this edge case results in a security bug instead
(crbug.com/550181232, crbug.com/566136673).

Since `mojo::DataPipeDrainer` is intended for general consumption, it
should handle this more gracefully instead of requiring all of its
callers to be careful. Add a WeakPtr guard and document the lifetime and
validity expectations.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Unregister permission usage observer from the original page
📋 Metadata
commit
2cfbba13cf3a60f4d915dbef2ea5d16949e42c0f
[ log ]
author
Jacek Oleksy <joleksy@opera.com>
Fri Oct 30 15:32:41 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 30 15:32:41 2026
tree
576c8834482207ad26d6149818578fcee5693137
parent
1bfccd2833837d7dc846d1094ee7349e7d5b958b [ diff ] 
Unregister permission usage observer from the original page

PageInfo registers itself as a PermissionUsageObserver on the
PageSpecificContentSettings of the primary main frame it was created
for, but its destructor looked up the *current* primary main frame to
unregister. When the user navigates while the Page Info bubble is open,
the bubble is destroyed after the primary main frame has already been
swapped, so RemovePermissionUsageObserver() was called on the new page's
PageSpecificContentSettings. If the original page went into the
back-forward cache, its PageSpecificContentSettings kept a dangling
observer, and the next permission usage notification after restoring
that page (e.g. OnContentBlocked()) hit the observer list CHECK.

Keep a WeakPtr to the PageSpecificContentSettings of the page PageInfo
was created for, and use it for both observer registration and all
reads, replacing GetPageSpecificContentSettings(). Observer removal and
the data shown in the bubble now stay on the page the bubble was opened
for. If that page is gone, its PageSpecificContentSettings is gone with
it and there is nothing left to read or unregister from.

Prevent PageContentCache from caching closing active tabs
📋 Metadata
commit
1bfccd2833837d7dc846d1094ee7349e7d5b958b
[ log ]
author

committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Fri Oct 30 15:31:26 2026
tree
10bf849386ff31b14a574b323bdbd4ffcc8999fc
parent
7639d33155fc3d87f5ae721a18b39835da337295 [ diff ] 
Prevent PageContentCache from caching closing active tabs

This is the first in a series of CLs implementing desktop tab lifecycle
handling for PageContentCache.

When an active tab is closed on desktop, TabModel::DestroyTabFeatures
runs before BrowserView unparents the closing WebContents and fires
OnVisibilityChanged(HIDDEN). Because AnnotatedPageContentRequest is
attached as WebContentsUserData, it outlives TabFeatures and still
receives that late hide notification. This means that
GetPageContentAnnotationsTabId still returns a valid SessionID during
tab teardown, causing PageContentCacheHandler to treat the closing
active tab as backgrounded and write its content to PageContentCache.

This change checks that TabInterface::MaybeGetFromContents and
GetTabFeatures are non-null in GetPageContentAnnotationsTabId,
returning std::nullopt once TabFeatures is destroyed so late
visibility changes during tab closure are ignored. Moving tab-scoped
cache handling into a TabFeatures-owned helper is left as a follow-up
TODO.

8e6ff02cc7a65600d39bd963d9c332cd28afdbd0 [ diff ] 
[sync] Introduce PendingEphemeralContainer for ephemeral key fetches

This patch introduces PendingEphemeralContainer, a short-lived helper
class that represents an ephemeral container while its initial
server-minted ephemeral key fetch is in flight (with exponential backoff
up to 5 attempts).

PendingEphemeralContainer generates a random container encryption key
immediately so callers can start compressing and encrypting page context
right away, queues encrypted item uploads and access token requests
until the fetch completes, and notifies its owner via a completion
callback with the generated key set and built access token before
flushing queued operations.

2ca61a0869830c4493417cf167dc4aa7d454805f [ diff ] 
[EVP] Fix blurry issuer favicon in popup on HiDPI displays

The Desktop Email Verification Protocol (EVP) permission popup requested
the issuer favicon at kDesiredIconSize (20) pixels and wrapped it as a
1x image. On displays with a device scale factor above 1 (e.g. 2x), the
20px bitmap was upscaled to fill the 20 DIP icon, so it looked blurry.

This CL requests the favicon at kDesiredIconSize DIPs times the device
scale factor of the display the popup is shown on (e.g. 40px on a 2x
display), and tags the decoded bitmap with that scale so it maps to 20
DIPs without being upscaled. GetIconRawBitmapOrFallbackStyleForPageUrl()
only returns a bitmap if the stored icon is at least the requested size
and returns just a fallback style otherwise, in which case the static
email-verified icon is kept instead of a blurry favicon.

Bug: 516844155
Test: unit_tests, manually on a 2x display

[OpenScreen] Add ADB receiver connectivity preflight in media perf

When the Chromecast receiver at 172.16.0.3:5555 is powered off or
offline, openscreen_cast_performance_test.py logs and ignores the failed
adb connect, installs Chrome on the sender, and then hangs for 300
seconds per video in enable_tab_mirroring() waiting for Cast discovery
to time out.

- Add verify_receiver_connectivity(receiver_addr, adb_path) and
  ReceiverUnreachableError / ReceiverAdbError in remote_transport.py.
- Check TCP port 5555, run adb connect, and verify adb -s <addr> shell
  echo ok, retrying up to 3 times 5s apart and disconnecting stale/
  offline ADB transports between attempts.
- Call verify_receiver_connectivity() in
  openscreen_cast_performance_test.py before Chrome setup, and call
  sender.verify_connectivity() before the Windows CPU probe in
  should_run_vp9_tests().

R=bmyersmorgan@google.com

9c495238d4911cbf6c89a4ff929e24c580c497ef

df8d5dbc010038085a105544d4dd87d0672a728b [ diff ] 
[omnibox][webui] Explain allow_exact_keyword_match instead of TODO

The TODO claimed `SearchboxHandler` should set
`AutocompleteInput::allow_exact_keyword_match` even outside keyword
mode, to match `OmniboxEditModel`. It shouldn't.

`allow_exact_keyword_match` lets autocomplete treat "<keyword> <query>"
as a keyword search: it enables the keyword's verbatim match as the
default and its suggestions. Outside keyword mode both Views and WebUI
pass false, so pasted text or text typed with space-triggering disabled
doesn't turn into a default keyword search (and, in Views, enter
keyword mode via `OnCurrentMatchChanged()`).

Views' extra `|| allow_exact_keyword_match_` term is a one-pass
transient set only when a space is typed in the middle of
"<keyword><query>". It lets that pass produce a default keyword match,
which then puts the model in keyword mode (SPACE_IN_MIDDLE). The WebUI
detects this case in `keyword_mode_manager.ts`, enters keyword mode
itself, and queries with the keyword, so `is_keyword_selected` is
already true here. The two surfaces agree in every reachable state.

Comment-only change; no behavior change.

TAG=agy
CONV=65cd30f1-8cfa-4ae0-8a37-be1051fe0426

72c36baba602b3821963f8baf5d693dbc222c262 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Wait for Autofill form cache in SaveCardInfobarEGTest

SaveCardInfobarEGTest fills and submits the card form right after the
page loads. If Autofill hasn't parsed the form yet,
`BrowserAutofillManager::ValidateSubmittedForm()` drops the submission,
no save-card flow starts, and `waitForEvents` times out. Since
crrev.com/c/8672170 added sign-in to `setUp`, this flakes often (4-6 of
19 tests per local run), and the retries slow down ios-simulator bots.

Wait for the form to be cached after loading it before filling, as
SaveProfileEGTest does (crrev.com/c/8065211). Loads that only navigate,
and the disabled xframe tests, are unchanged.

[IOS][AIM] Remove ThreadContextLibrary capability and item source

Assistant AIM on iOS now maintains and transfers attached tabs natively
through ContextualSearchSessionHandle, so hydrating webpage chips from
UpdateThreadContextLibrary is no longer needed and would duplicate
transferred tabs.

Stop advertising THREAD_CONTEXT_LIBRARY during the handshake, ignore
UpdateThreadContextLibrary messages, and remove the unused
kContextLibrary item source plumbing.

Bug: 579860003

[ContextualTasks] Add dedicated avatar tooltip string

The avatar tooltip in the Contextual Tasks side panel toolbar reuses
the password manager's "Google Account" link string, because the
feature missed the M158 string cutoff. That string's translator
context describes a saved-passwords link, so translations can drift
from the tooltip's meaning.

Add a dedicated Contextual Tasks string with its translation
screenshot, switch the tooltip to it, and drop the stopgap TODOs.

Fixed: 578985445

f17347d40d72d86d69712af2d1d910345bd0fe33 [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Re-enable OAuth retry unit test

`GetAccessToken_TransientError_Retries` was previously disabled on Linux
ASan due to a heap-use-after-free during `RenderViewHostTestHarness`
teardown when shutting down shared D-Bus instances bound to an earlier
test's thread pool. Now that `DBusThreadLinux` owns a dedicated D-Bus
thread and unit tests no longer watch host screen lock over D-Bus, the
teardown race no longer occurs and the test can run across all
configurations.

Fixed: 477018818

60cf978a88d18dab8cc29ca95d7cb9f9daabd7df [ diff ] 
[DeepCAF] Load custom form control scripts into an isolated world

Add CustomFormControlScriptExecutor, a RenderFrameObserver that runs
the custom form control script of the current document in an isolated
world, so that the script can access the frame's DOM without sharing
globals with the page. The creator of the executor passes the world
id; Chrome will pass ISOLATED_WORLD_ID_AUTOFILL_CUSTOM_FORM_CONTROLS,
which //components can't include.

A script declares createCustomFormControlManager(), which returns the
script's manager: an object whose methods detect, read and fill the
page's custom form controls (e.g. select-like widgets that aren't
<select> elements). LoadScript() runs the script and keeps the manager
until the isolated world's script context is released, e.g. when a new
document is committed or the frame is detached, so that the manager
doesn't keep the context alive. The script is wrapped in a function so
that its top-level declarations stay local instead of becoming globals
of the isolated world.

The executor is owned by its creator, not by the frame. Running a
script may detach the frame (e.g. through the page's event handlers
that the script triggers), and the creator may then destroy the
executor while the script runs. LoadScript() detects this with a
WeakPtr and fails without using the executor afterwards.

Nothing creates the executor yet. Follow-up CLs harden how scripts are
loaded, lock down the isolated world, add calls into the manager, limit
how long scripts may run, and connect the executor to the browser.

Bug: b:578383598
Test: components_browsertests --gtest_filter=CustomFormControlScriptExecutorTest.*
